[[sec-212-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `smc_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable = !feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test || !feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug || !feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` — any required enable low disables the SMC OTP bridge
* When gated, SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) falls back to BYPASS (1-bit DR) and no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Independent gating by driving each of `fuse_test`, `soc_debug`, and `ap_debug` low, then full restoration after restoring enables high
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after restoration (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all three required enables high, issue one SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x1C, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate via each control, one at a time:
a. Drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 0; load IR=0x1C and attempt a write; verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) and no transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`; restore and verify normal operation.
b. Repeat for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug` = 0.
c. Repeat for `feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test` = 0.
. Final restore check:
a. With all required enables high, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_bypass_chk` |With any required enable low, IR=0x1C selects BYPASS (1-bit DR)
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each required enable is restored
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `fuse_test`, `soc_debug`, or `ap_debug` driven low the write is gated to BYPASS with no AXI-Lite traffic, and restoring each enable high restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-213-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_test]]
===== 2.13 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single write

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op AXI4-Lite write through the SEP OTP bridge (SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x22)
* 32-bit address / 32-bit data field formatting consistent with the SEP OTP CAPS TDR (IR=0x21, AXI4-Lite, 32/32)
* Address/data/wstrb delivery to the AXI-Lite subordinate on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Op-status transitions through Busy (0b11) and settles at OKAY (0b00)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load and verify the bridge CAPS:
a. Load SEP_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x21) via `OcahJtagTap` and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
b. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite, ADDR_SIZE = 32, DATA_SIZE = 32.
. Issue the single write:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. Shift in the 32-bit target address, 32-bit write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
. Poll for completion:
a. Re-enter Capture-DR/Shift-DR and read the op-status field until not Busy (0b11).
b. Verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the side effect:
a. Confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate received the write at the target address with the expected data and wstrb.
b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4-Lite, 32-bit address/data
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status passes through Busy then returns OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_data_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The AXI4-Lite single write completes with OKAY status and the SEP OTP subordinate captures the correct address and data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-213a-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.13a `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI-Lite single write delivers the expected 32-bit data word
* Read-back verifies byte-lane placement and final memory-visible value
* Write/read operations both return OKAY status

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SEP OTP AXI-Lite single write with a non-zero 32-bit pattern and valid write strobes.
. Poll until write status is OKAY.
. Read the same SEP OTP address through the bridge.
. Compare the returned word against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written SEP OTP word
|`sep_otp_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SEP OTP written word is returned by bridge read-back with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-214-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge with incrementing 32-bit addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* Per-beat op-status reaching OKAY (0b00) before advancing the address
* Distinct data pattern per address landing at the correct AXI-Lite subordinate location (no aliasing)
* Address advance consistent with the 32-bit data geometry

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a base address, a 32-bit-word stride, and a beat count N.
b. Build a per-beat data pattern and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift in address = base {plus} i*stride, the i-th data word, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. After each Update-DR, poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the resulting memory image:
a. For each address in the series, confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the expected data.
b. Confirm no writes landed outside the addressed range.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |Every beat returns OKAY (0b00) after Busy
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |Captured AW addresses follow base {plus} i*stride with no aliasing
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct expected data pattern
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete with OKAY status and each target address holds its expected data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-215-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge all targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no-incr mode)
* Each beat re-uses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one AXI-Lite subordinate location
* Per-beat op-status reaching OKAY (0b00) for every repeated write
* Final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single target address and a sequence of N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift in the same target address, the i-th data word, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. After each Update-DR, poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm all N writes targeted exactly the one fixed address on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
b. Confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the last beat's data at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |Each repeated write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |All captured AW addresses equal the single fixed address
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds the final (last-beat) data
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete with OKAY status and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-216-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4-Lite write series in which selected beats target an error-generating region of the SEP OTP subordinate
* The non-OKAY op-status path (0b01 SLVERR / 0b10 DECERR) surfacing in the op-status TDR for erroring beats
* OKAY and erroring beats interleaved, confirming the bridge does not hang or corrupt state after an error
* Recovery: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY after an erroring beat

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the error stimulus:
a. Program the AXI-Lite subordinate (or its wrapper) to return a non-OKAY response for a designated error region.
b. Choose an incrementing series in which some beats fall inside the error region and some are legal.
. Issue the series and capture per-beat status:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each beat: shift in address = base {plus} i*stride, data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and record the op-status field.
d. For legal beats verify op = 0b00 (OKAY); for error beats verify op reflects the error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR).
. Confirm containment and recovery:
a. Issue a final legal write to a clean address and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
b. Verify legal-beat data landed correctly and no spurious writes resulted from the erroring beats.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_err_status_chk` |Error beats report SLVERR/DECERR; legal beats report OKAY
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_err_data_chk` |Only legal-beat addresses are updated in the subordinate
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_err_recovery_chk` |A post-error legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Erroring beats report the expected non-OKAY status, legal beats and the post-error write complete with OKAY, and memory matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-217-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.17 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge over randomized address, data, and wstrb
* Stress of the 32-bit address / 32-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A reference memory mirroring every issued write, compared against the AXI-Lite subordinate at the end
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random in-range 32-bit address, random data, random legal wstrb) for the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
b. Apply each accepted write to a reference memory model.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data, wstrb, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Inspect the AXI-Lite subordinate and compare every written location against the reference memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Final subordinate image matches the reference memory exactly
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and the final subordinate memory image matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-218-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `sep_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable = !feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test || !feat_ctrl_i.sep_debug || !feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug || !feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` — any required enable low disables the SEP OTP bridge
* When gated, SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) falls back to BYPASS (1-bit DR) and no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Independent gating by driving each of `fuse_test`, `sep_debug`, `soc_debug`, and `ap_debug` low, then full restoration after restoring enables high
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after restoration (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all four required enables high, issue one SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x22, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate via each control, one at a time:
a. Drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 0; load IR=0x22 and attempt a write; verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) and no transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`; restore and verify normal operation.
b. Repeat for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug` = 0.
c. Repeat for `feat_ctrl_i.sep_debug` = 0.
d. Repeat for `feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test` = 0.
. Final restore check:
a. With all required enables high, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_bypass_chk` |With any required enable low, IR=0x22 selects BYPASS (1-bit DR)
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each required enable is restored
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `fuse_test`, `sep_debug`, `soc_debug`, or `ap_debug` driven low the write is gated to BYPASS with no AXI-Lite traffic, and restoring each enable high restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios]]
==== 3. JTAG2AXI Read Scenarios

[[sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric AXI4 single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) DR field packing: address (56-bit), data, wstrb, size, op
* Read op encoding `op=0b01` with data=0 and wstrb=0, and busy/terminal status polling
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by `OcahAxiRam`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4, addr_size = 56, data_size = 64.
. Issue a single write through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge to a chosen target address.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, size, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Reload SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11), then read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the previously written value and the `OcahAxiRam` reference.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the `OcahAxiRam` model
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiRam` content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential SMC fabric AXI4 accesses across a block of incrementing addresses
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) reuse across a series with per-op address advance
* Per-beat read data integrity against an incrementing pattern in `OcahAxiRam`
* Stable OKAY terminal status across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build an incrementing reference pattern in `OcahAxiRam` over a contiguous SMC fabric address block.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. For each address, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in address{plus}i*stride, unique data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
b. Poll each op until not Busy; verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each address, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in address{plus}i*stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status.
. Compare every returned beat with the `OcahAxiRam` reference and the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_status_chk` |Every op in the series reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the incrementing-address reference value
|`smc_axi_series_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiRam` content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes OKAY and all per-beat comparisons match the reference memory, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-33-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated SMC fabric AXI4 reads/writes targeting one fixed address (no address advance)
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) reissue with an unchanged address field
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated ops

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single fixed SMC fabric target address and seed it in `OcahAxiRam`.
. Write the chosen value once through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the fixed address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read the same fixed address repeatedly through the bridge.
a. For each iteration, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the fixed address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status.
. Verify every captured beat equals the single repeated expected value.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_status_chk` |Every fixed-address op reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the single repeated expected value
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiRam` at the fixed address remains the written value across the series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address ops complete OKAY and every returned beat equals the repeated expected value, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-34-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric AXI4 incrementing series that intersects an error-generating subordinate region
* Error response capture in the op[1:0] status (SLVERR 0b01 / DECERR 0b10)
* Bridge does not hang, corrupt the DR, or lose state after a non-OKAY response
* Recovery: a subsequent legal read to a good address completes OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure `OcahAxiRam` (or its error wrapper) so a subset of the incrementing block returns SLVERR/DECERR.
. Issue the incrementing read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. For each address, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in address{plus}i*stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status per op.
. For addresses in the error region, verify op reflects the expected error code (0b01/0b10).
. For addresses in the good region, verify op=0b00 (OKAY) and correct data.
. After the error ops, issue one more legal read to a good address and confirm OKAY and correct data (recovery).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_incr_error_status_chk` |Error-region ops report the expected SLVERR/DECERR op code
|`smc_axi_incr_error_rdata_chk` |Good-region beats match the reference; error beats are not mistaken for valid data
|`smc_axi_incr_error_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiRam` good-region content matches expectations
|`smc_axi_incr_error_robustness_chk` |Bridge recovers and a post-error legal read completes OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each op reaches its expected terminal status (OKAY or the configured error), the bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC fabric AXI4 read addresses and sizes against a randomized reference
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) field packing under randomized address/size stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload `OcahAxiRam` with a randomized reference image over the SMC fabric read window.
. Generate a constrained-random sequence of in-range read addresses (and legal sizes).
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the `OcahAxiRam` reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized `OcahAxiRam` reference
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `smc_jtag2axi_security_disable = !soc_debug || !ap_debug` independently gates the SMC fabric read bridge when a required enable is low
* Gated JTAG2AXI instructions fall back to BYPASS (1-bit DR); no AXI traffic on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`
* Normal read operation restored after the required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read returning OKAY with expected data from `OcahAxiRam`.
. Drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug=0`; load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and attempt a read.
a. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (DR length 1 bit) and no AXI transaction issues on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Restore `ap_debug=1`; verify normal read restored.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug=0`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_bypass_chk` |DR is 1-bit BYPASS while `ap_debug` or `soc_debug` is low
|`smc_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the bit clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each required enable independently forces BYPASS with no AXI traffic when low, and reads resume correctly once restored high, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test]]
===== 3.6a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating with explicit no-AXI-activity check

*Test Coverage*:

* `soc_debug` and `ap_debug` gating prevent any SMC fabric read request from reaching AXI
* No `AWVALID` or `ARVALID` activity occurs while the bridge is gated
* Normal read activity resumes after the required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read and confirm AXI activity is observed.
. Drive one required enable low (`ap_debug`, then `soc_debug`) and attempt a SMC fabric read.
. Monitor AXI request channels for a bounded window and verify no AW/AR valid activity occurs.
. Restore the required enable and verify a normal read again produces AXI activity and OKAY status.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_no_aw_ar_chk` |No AWVALID or ARVALID while the bridge is gated
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_activity_chk` |AXI read activity resumes after gating clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each required enable independently blocks all SMC fabric AXI read activity while low and normal reads resume after restore, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-37-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential SMC OTP AXI4-Lite accesses across a block of incrementing 32-bit addresses
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) reuse across a series with per-op address advance
* Per-beat read data integrity against an incrementing pattern in the AXI-Lite subordinate
* Stable OKAY terminal status across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build an incrementing reference pattern in the SMC OTP AXI-Lite subordinate over a contiguous address block.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. For each address, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in address{plus}i*stride, unique data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
b. Poll each op until not Busy; verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each address, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in address{plus}i*stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status.
. Compare every returned beat with the subordinate reference and the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_series_incr_status_chk` |Every op in the series reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_otp_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the incrementing-address reference value
|`smc_otp_series_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes OKAY and all per-beat comparisons match the reference memory, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-39-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated SMC OTP AXI4-Lite reads/writes targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no address advance)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) reissue with an unchanged address field
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated ops

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single fixed SMC OTP target address and seed it in the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Write the chosen value once through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in the fixed address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read the same fixed address repeatedly through the bridge.
a. For each iteration, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in the fixed address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status.
. Verify every captured beat equals the single repeated expected value.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_status_chk` |Every fixed-address op reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the single repeated expected value
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate at the fixed address remains the written value across the series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address ops complete OKAY and every returned beat equals the repeated expected value, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-310-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite incrementing series that intersects an error-generating subordinate region
* Error response capture in the op[1:0] status (SLVERR 0b01 / DECERR 0b10)
* Bridge does not hang, corrupt the DR, or lose state after a non-OKAY response
* Recovery: a subsequent legal read to a good address completes OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the SMC OTP AXI-Lite subordinate so a subset of the incrementing block returns SLVERR/DECERR.
. Issue the incrementing read series through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. For each address, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in address{plus}i*stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status per op.
. For addresses in the error region, verify op reflects the expected error code (0b01/0b10).
. For addresses in the good region, verify op=0b00 (OKAY) and correct data.
. After the error ops, issue one more legal read to a good address and confirm OKAY and correct data (recovery).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_incr_error_status_chk` |Error-region ops report the expected SLVERR/DECERR op code
|`smc_otp_incr_error_rdata_chk` |Good-region beats match the reference; error beats are not mistaken for valid data
|`smc_otp_incr_error_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate good-region content matches expectations
|`smc_otp_incr_error_robustness_chk` |Bridge recovers and a post-error legal read completes OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each op reaches its expected terminal status (OKAY or the configured error), the bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

'''''

[[sec-312-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `smc_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable = !fuse_test || !soc_debug || !ap_debug` independently gates the SMC OTP read bridge
* Gated JTAG2AXI instructions fall back to BYPASS (1-bit DR); no AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Normal read operation restored after each required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC OTP read returning OKAY with expected data from the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug=0`; load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and attempt a read.
a. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (DR length 1 bit) and no AXI-Lite transaction issues on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Restore `ap_debug=1`; verify normal read restored.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug=0`.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test=0`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description

|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each of `fuse_test`, `soc_debug`, and `ap_debug` independently forces BYPASS with no AXI-Lite traffic when low, and reads resume correctly once restored high, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 3.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI4-Lite incrementing series that intersects an error-generating subordinate region
* Error response capture in the op[1:0] status (SLVERR 0b01 / DECERR 0b10)
* Bridge does not hang, corrupt the DR, or lose state after a non-OKAY response
* Recovery: a subsequent legal read to a good address completes OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the SEP OTP AXI-Lite subordinate so a subset of the incrementing block returns SLVERR/DECERR.
. Issue the incrementing read series through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. For each address, load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22); shift in address{plus}i*stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status per op.
. For addresses in the error region, verify op reflects the expected error code (0b01/0b10).
. For addresses in the good region, verify op=0b00 (OKAY) and correct data.
. After the error ops, issue one more legal read to a good address and confirm OKAY and correct data (recovery).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_incr_error_status_chk` |Error-region ops report the expected SLVERR/DECERR op code
|`sep_otp_incr_error_rdata_chk` |Good-region beats match the reference; error beats are not mistaken for valid data
|`sep_otp_incr_error_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate good-region content matches expectations
|`sep_otp_incr_error_robustness_chk` |Bridge recovers and a post-error legal read completes OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each op reaches its expected terminal status (OKAY or the configured error), the bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-317-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.17 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SEP OTP AXI4-Lite read addresses against a randomized 32-bit reference
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) field packing under randomized address stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload the SEP OTP AXI-Lite subordinate with a randomized reference image over the read window.
. Generate a constrained-random sequence of in-range 32-bit read addresses.
. Issue each randomized read through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the AXI-Lite subordinate reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`sep_otp_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized AXI-Lite reference
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-318-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `sep_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable = !fuse_test || !sep_debug || !soc_debug || !ap_debug` independently gates the SEP OTP read bridge
* Gated JTAG2AXI instructions fall back to BYPASS (1-bit DR); no AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Normal read operation restored after each required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SEP OTP read returning OKAY with expected data from the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug=0`; load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) and attempt a read.
a. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (DR length 1 bit) and no AXI-Lite transaction issues on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Restore `ap_debug=1`; verify normal read restored.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug=0`.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.sep_debug=0`.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test=0`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_rd_gate_bypass_chk` |DR is 1-bit BYPASS while `ap_debug`, `soc_debug`, `sep_debug`, or `fuse_test` is low
|`sep_otp_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI-Lite transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after each bit clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each of `fuse_test`, `sep_debug`, `soc_debug`, and `ap_debug` independently forces BYPASS with no AXI-Lite traffic when low, and reads resume correctly once restored high, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-4-jtag2axi-error-backpressure-cdc-and-decode-scenarios]]
==== 4. JTAG2AXI Error, Backpressure, CDC, and Decode Scenarios

[[sec-41-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC fabric AXI4 debug bridge (SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating region
* AXI4 subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (BRESP = SLVERR or DECERR) captured in op[1:0] (0b10 / 0b01)
* No spurious or partial write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`
* Bridge does not hang or corrupt internal state; a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 subordinate (OcahAxiRam) to return SLVERR or DECERR on writes to the chosen error region.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x28):
a. Shift in the error-region target address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, size, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status field via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (op != 0b11).
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR) and not OKAY (0b00).
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the AXI subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data/wstrb at the subordinate, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious memory update occurs, and the bridge recovers for the next legal transaction, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-42-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_read_test]]
===== 4.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op read through the SMC fabric AXI4 debug bridge (IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating region
* AXI4 subordinate returns a non-OKAY read response (RRESP = SLVERR or DECERR) captured in op[1:0] (0b01 / 0b10)
* Read-data field is ignored or held at the bridge-defined value when the response is non-OKAY
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 subordinate to return SLVERR or DECERR on reads to the error region.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x28):
a. Shift in the error-region target address, data = 0, wstrb = 0, size, op = 0b01 (Read).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 read on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code and that the read-data field is treated as invalid (ignored / defined value).
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference value; verify OKAY and matching read data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_status_chk` |Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read data is ignored/held to the defined value on error
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the read-data field is handled per spec, and the bridge recovers for the next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-43-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric no-increment write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat; address mode remains fixed for every beat
* Op-status identifies the failing beat while good beats commit correctly to the fixed address
* Bridge recovers: the series can be re-run or a new legal access completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response on a selected beat at the fixed target address.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and program a fixed-address (no address increment) write series of N beats.
. Run the series via repeated Update-DR launches; the bridge holds the AXI address constant across beats.
. Poll the op-status after each beat (and the final op) until not Busy.
. Verify the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR and that good beats committed correct data to the fixed address; confirm the AXI address never incremented on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing beat with the injected error code
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit to the fixed address; no address increment observed
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The injected fault is reported on the failing beat, the address stays fixed for all beats, good beats commit correctly, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-44-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric no-increment read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat while the AXI address remains fixed
* Good beats return the reference value at the fixed address; failing beat marks invalid data
* Op-status error-information matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with a known value at the fixed address and a non-OKAY response on a selected read beat.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and program a fixed-address read series of N beats.
. Run the series; the bridge holds the AXI read address constant across beats.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats return the reference data, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR with read data treated as invalid, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing read beat with the injected error code
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the fixed-address reference; failing beat data is invalid
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the fixed-address reference, the failing beat reports the injected error, the address stays fixed, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-45-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28)
* One beat in the auto-incrementing sequence hits an error region and returns SLVERR/DECERR
* Good beats before/after the fault commit to their correctly-incremented addresses
* Op-status identifies the failing beat; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and program an incrementing-address write series of N beats with a known base address and stride.
. Run the series; the bridge advances the AXI address by the stride each beat.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR, and the address progression on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing beat in the incrementing series
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats commit at the expected incrementing addresses, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-46-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28)
* One address in the auto-incrementing range returns SLVERR/DECERR mid-series
* Good beats before the fault return reference data at their incremented addresses
* Op-status error-information matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with reference data across the address range and configure a non-OKAY response at one address.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and program an incrementing-address read series of N beats with a known base and stride.
. Run the series; the bridge advances the AXI read address each beat.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats return the reference data at their incremented addresses, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR with invalid data, and the address progression matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing read beat in the incrementing series
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the expected incrementing-address references, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test]]
===== 4.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing write with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28) with a fault mid-series
* After the series completes, the sticky error-status TDR is scanned out to retrieve the latched fault information
* Error-status TDR identifies the failing beat/address and the SLVERR/DECERR code without per-beat polling
* Sticky status clears as defined; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and run an incrementing-address write series of N beats.
. Allow the series to run to completion; do not stop on the failing beat.
. Scan out the sticky error-status TDR via Capture-DR/Shift-DR and decode the latched failing-beat index, address, and SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses and the latched status matches the injected fault.
. Clear the sticky status as defined, then issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_tdr_chk` |Error-status TDR latches the failing beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_recover_chk` |Sticky status clears and a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The sticky error-status TDR correctly reports the failing beat and error code, good beats commit, the status clears, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-48-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test]]
===== 4.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing read with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge (IR=0x28) with a fault mid-series
* Sticky error-status TDR scanned out after the series to retrieve the latched failing-beat fault
* Good beats before the fault return reference data; error-status TDR identifies the failing beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code
* Sticky status clears as defined; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with reference data and configure a non-OKAY response at one address in the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and run an incrementing-address read series of N beats to completion.
. Scan out the sticky error-status TDR and decode the latched failing-beat index, address, and SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented addresses and the latched status matches the injected fault.
. Clear the sticky status, then issue a follow-up legal read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_tdr_chk` |Error-status TDR latches the failing read beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_recover_chk` |Sticky status clears and a subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The sticky error-status TDR correctly reports the failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, the status clears, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-49-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_security_gating_test]]
===== 4.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric bridge gating: `smc_jtag2axi_security_disable = !feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug || !feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug`
* When gated, SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) falls back to BYPASS (1-bit DR) and issues no AXI traffic on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, even on error-path attempts
* Each required enable low (`soc_debug`, `ap_debug`) disables the bridge; restoring it high restores operation
* Error-path read/write attempts while gated produce no subordinate access and no error-status update

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With the subordinate configured to return errors on the target region, drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 0 to gate the bridge.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and attempt an error-region read and write:
a. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR).
b. Verify no AXI transaction is issued on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` and no error-status latches.
. Restore `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 1; verify a normal (and error-path) JTAG2AXI access resumes and the error is reported as expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 with `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug` = 0 to confirm it independently gates the bridge.
. Confirm that with all required enables high, ungated error-path accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_axi_err_gating_bypass_chk` |Gated instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) with `soc_debug` or `ap_debug` set
|`smc_axi_err_gating_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI traffic on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_axi_err_gating_restore_chk` |Restoring each required enable restores normal/error-path operation
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Either required enable low forces BYPASS with no AXI traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each enable is restored, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-410-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge (SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x1C) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate (AXI-Lite wrapper) to return SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x1C):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-411-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_read_test]]
===== 4.11 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op read through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY read response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* Read-data field is ignored or held at the bridge-defined value on a non-OKAY response
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return SLVERR/DECERR on reads to the error region.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x1C):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, data = 0, wstrb = 0, op = 0b01 (Read).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite read on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code and the read-data field is treated as invalid.
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference value; verify OKAY and matching data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_status_chk` |Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read data is ignored/held to the defined value on error
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the read-data field is handled per spec, and the bridge recovers for the next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-412-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C)
* One beat in the auto-incrementing sequence returns SLVERR/DECERR
* Good beats before/after the fault commit to their correctly-incremented addresses
* Op-status identifies the failing beat; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and program an incrementing-address write series of N beats with a known base and stride.
. Run the series; the bridge advances the AXI-Lite address each beat on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR, and the address progression matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing beat in the incrementing series
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats commit at the expected incrementing addresses, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-413-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.13 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C)
* One address in the auto-incrementing range returns SLVERR/DECERR mid-series
* Good beats before the fault return reference data at their incremented addresses
* Op-status error-information matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with reference data across the range and configure a non-OKAY response at one address.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and program an incrementing-address read series of N beats with a known base and stride.
. Run the series; the bridge advances the AXI-Lite read address each beat.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats return the reference data at their incremented addresses, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR with invalid data, and the address progression matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing read beat in the incrementing series
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the expected incrementing-address references, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-414-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP no-increment write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat; AXI-Lite address remains fixed for every beat
* Op-status identifies the failing beat while good beats commit correctly to the fixed address
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response on a selected beat at the fixed target address.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and program a fixed-address write series of N beats.
. Run the series; the bridge holds the AXI-Lite address constant across beats on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR, good beats committed correctly to the fixed address, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing beat with the injected error code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit to the fixed address; no address increment observed
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The injected fault is reported on the failing beat, the address stays fixed, good beats commit correctly, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-415-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP no-increment read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) read series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat while the AXI-Lite address remains fixed
* Good beats return the reference value at the fixed address; failing beat marks invalid data
* Op-status error-information matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with a known value at the fixed address and a non-OKAY response on a selected read beat.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and program a fixed-address read series of N beats.
. Run the series; the bridge holds the AXI-Lite read address constant across beats.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.
. Verify good beats return the reference data, the failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR with invalid data, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_status_chk` |Op-status flags the failing read beat with the injected error code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the fixed-address reference; failing beat data is invalid
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the fixed-address reference, the failing beat reports the injected error, the address stays fixed, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test]]
===== 4.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C) with a fault mid-series
* Sticky error-status TDR scanned out after the series to retrieve the latched failing-beat fault
* Error-status TDR identifies the failing beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code without per-beat polling
* Sticky status clears as defined; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and run an incrementing-address write series of N beats to completion.
. Scan out the sticky error-status TDR via Capture-DR/Shift-DR and decode the latched failing-beat index, address, and SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses and the latched status matches the injected fault.
. Clear the sticky status, then issue a follow-up legal write; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_tdr_chk` |Error-status TDR latches the failing beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_recover_chk` |Sticky status clears and a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The sticky error-status TDR correctly reports the failing beat and error code, good beats commit, the status clears, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-417-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test]]

. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and run an incrementing-address read series of N beats to completion.
. Scan out the sticky error-status TDR and decode the latched failing-beat index, address, and SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented addresses and the latched status matches the injected fault.

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===

|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_recover_chk` |Sticky status clears and a subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The sticky error-status TDR correctly reports the failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, the status clears, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-418-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test]]
===== 4.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP bridge gating: `smc_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable = !feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test || !feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug || !feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug`
* When gated, SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) falls back to BYPASS (1-bit DR) and issues no AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, even on error-path attempts
* Each required enable low (`fuse_test`, `soc_debug`, `ap_debug`) disables the bridge; restoring it high restores operation
* Error-path read/write attempts while gated produce no subordinate access and no error-status update

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With the AXI-Lite subordinate configured to return errors on the target region, drive required enable `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 0 to gate the bridge.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and attempt an error-region read and write:
a. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR).
b. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction is issued on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` and no error-status latches.
. Restore `feat_ctrl_i.ap_debug` = 1; verify normal/error-path operation resumes and the error is reported as expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 for `feat_ctrl_i.soc_debug` = 0 and `feat_ctrl_i.fuse_test` = 0, confirming each independently gates the bridge.
. Confirm that with all required enables high, ungated error-path accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_bypass_chk` |Gated instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) for each of `fuse_test`/`soc_debug`/`ap_debug`
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_restore_chk` |Restoring each required enable restores normal/error-path operation
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Any required enable low forces BYPASS with no AXI-Lite traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each enable is restored, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-419-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.19 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge (SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x22) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x22):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''
