'''''

[[sec-613-dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.13 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CLA to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between internal CLA channels (source CLA to multiple destination CLA)
* `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` programmed with multiple internal CLA destination bits
* All selected destination CLA channels pulse on the source CLA pulse; non-selected stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition of two CLA sources sharing a destination CLA channel
* No feedback loop where a destination CLA re-triggers its own source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the intra-CLA multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` with several CLA destination bits set.
b. Read the register back and confirm the mask matches.
. Pulse the source CLA via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destination CLA channels with the OCAH-local CTM monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CLA pulses.
b. Verify the routing mask behavior: non-selected CLA channels stay idle and the source is not fed back to itself.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CLA source sharing one destination and confirm OR behavior on the shared channel.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_csr_chk` |CSR readback of the multicast `CT_DST_SELECT` mask matches the programmed value
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_route_chk` |All selected destination CLA channels pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_or_chk` |Overlapping CLA sources OR together at the shared CLA destination
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CLA
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA source multicasts to exactly its selected CLA destinations with correct wire-OR superposition and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-614-dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.14 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CTP to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between external CTP ports (source CTP to multiple destination CTP)
* `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` programmed with multiple external CTP destination bits
* All selected destination CTP ports pulse with matching pulse width on the source CTP pulse
* Wire-OR superposition of two CTP sources sharing a destination CTP port
* No feedback loop from a destination CTP port back into the source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the inter-CTP multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` with several CTP destination bits set.
b. Read the register back and confirm the mask matches.
. Pulse the source CTP via the OCAH-local CTP BFM.
. Observe the destination CTP ports with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CTP port pulses.
b. Verify pulse width on each destination matches the reference model.
c. Verify no feedback loop re-triggers the source.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CTP source sharing one destination and confirm OR behavior.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of the multicast `CT_DST_SELECT` mask matches the programmed value
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_route_chk` |All selected destination CTP ports pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_width_chk` |Destination CTP pulse width matches the source pulse
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CTP
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CTP source multicasts to exactly its selected CTP destinations with correct pulse width, wire-OR superposition, and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-615-dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.15 `dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_p2p_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: P2P CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM point-to-point routing of an internal CLA source to a single external CTP destination
* `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` programmed with exactly one destination bit set
* Request appears only at the selected CTP destination; no spurious triggers elsewhere
* Several distinct CLA-source / CTP-destination pairs exercised to vary the single-bit position

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the point-to-point route via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[src]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << ctp_dst)+` so a single CTP destination bit is set.
b. Read the register back and confirm only the one destination bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the CLA source via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destinations with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify the request appears at exactly the selected CTP destination.
b. Verify no other destination port observes the request.
. Repeat for several CLA-source / CTP-destination combinations to move the single set bit across the destination field.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback shows exactly one CTP destination bit set
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |Trigger reaches exactly the selected CTP destination against the reference model
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No spurious triggers on non-selected destinations
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA-to-CTP trigger is delivered only to the configured destination across all exercised pairs with no spurious activity, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-619-dtp_ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_test]]
===== 6.19 `dtp_ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset in wire-OR mode

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears all CTM wire-OR routing registers to `0x0000_0000`
* No spurious cross-trigger requests on any destination during or after reset
* CTM accepts new wire-OR configuration after reset and routes correctly
* Pre-reset multicast routing and active trigger traffic do not survive the reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish pre-reset wire-OR state:
a. Program one or more `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` with multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`.
b. Drive source pulses and confirm the multicast destinations respond.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) while wire-OR routes are active, then wait for reset deassertion.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`.
b. Confirm the OCAH-local CTM/CTP monitors observe no spurious trigger on any destination during or after reset.
. Reconfigure a fresh wire-OR route post-reset, pulse the source, and verify correct multicast routing resumes.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` after reset
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_nospur_chk` |No spurious trigger on any destination during or after reset
|`ctm_reset_wire_or_mode_route_chk` |Post-reset wire-OR reconfiguration routes correctly against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Reset clears all wire-OR routing state with no spurious destination toggles, and post-reset multicast routing matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-620-dtp_ctm_reset_p2p_mode_test]]
===== 6.20 `dtp_ctm_reset_p2p_mode_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset in P2P mode

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears all CTM point-to-point routing registers to `0x0000_0000`
* In-flight point-to-point handshake state is cleared by reset
* No spurious cross-trigger requests on any destination during or after reset
* A post-reset point-to-point transaction completes correctly

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish pre-reset P2P state:
a. Program one or more `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` with single-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`.
b. Start a point-to-point handshake on a configured source.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) mid-handshake, then wait for deassertion.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`.
b. Confirm the in-flight handshake state is cleared and the monitors observe no spurious trigger during or after reset.
. Reprogram a single-bit P2P route post-reset, drive the source, and verify the transaction completes only at the selected destination.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` after reset
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_nospur_chk` |No spurious trigger and no stale handshake state after reset
|`ctm_reset_p2p_mode_route_chk` |Post-reset point-to-point transaction completes at the selected destination
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Reset clears all point-to-point routing and handshake state with no spurious activity, and a post-reset P2P transaction completes correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-621-dtp_ctm_reset_all_modes_test]]
===== 6.21 `dtp_ctm_reset_all_modes_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: CTM reset across all modes

*Test Coverage*:

* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears CTM routing state programmed across both wire-OR and point-to-point modes
* All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` return to `0x0000_0000` default regardless of pre-reset mode mix
* No spurious cross-trigger requests on any destination during or after reset
* Both wire-OR and point-to-point routes can be reprogrammed and route correctly post-reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a mixed pre-reset configuration:
a. Program some sources with multi-bit (wire-OR) `CT_DST_SELECT` masks and others with single-bit (P2P) masks via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`.
b. Drive source pulses across both modes and confirm correct routing before reset.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`) while both route classes are active, then wait for deassertion.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
a. Read all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` and confirm each is `0x0000_0000`.
b. Confirm the monitors observe no spurious trigger on any destination during or after reset.
. Reprogram both a wire-OR and a point-to-point route post-reset, pulse the sources, and verify each routes correctly.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_csr_chk` |All `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` read back `0x0000_0000` after reset regardless of pre-reset mode
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_nospur_chk` |No spurious trigger on any destination during or after reset
|`ctm_reset_all_modes_route_chk` |Post-reset wire-OR and P2P reconfiguration both route correctly against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Reset returns all routing state to defaults for both modes with no spurious activity, and both wire-OR and P2P routes reprogram correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-622-dtp_ctm_rand_all_scenarios_test]]
===== 6.22 `dtp_ctm_rand_all_scenarios_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM all scenarios

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized `CT_DST_SELECT` masks across all CTM sources spanning both wire-OR and P2P routing
* Random source/destination pairs and trigger timing across CLA and CTP classes
* All observed routing matches the CTM reference model for every random configuration
* No routing corruption or state leakage between successive random configurations
* Wire-OR superposition correct whenever random masks overlap on a destination

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations (e.g. 20):
a. Generate random `CT_DST_SELECT` values for all CTM sources, mixing single-bit and multi-bit masks across CLA and CTP destinations.
b. Write all `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back to confirm the programmed mask.
c. Drive random cross-trigger requests on random source ports via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs with randomized timing.
d. Verify each request appears at exactly its configured destinations and that overlapping destinations OR correctly.
e. Confirm no residual routing carries over from the previous iteration's configuration.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_csr_chk` |Every randomized `CT_DST_SELECT` reads back as programmed
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_route_chk` |All observed routing matches the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_or_chk` |Overlapping random masks OR correctly at shared destinations
|`ctm_rand_all_scenarios_noleak_chk` |No routing state leaks between successive configurations
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random mixed-mode configuration routes exactly as modeled with correct wire-OR superposition and no inter-configuration leakage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-623-dtp_ctm_rand_wire_or_only_test]]
===== 6.23 `dtp_ctm_rand_wire_or_only_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM wire-OR only

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks restricted to wire-OR (multicast) routing only
* Random source pulses fan out to all selected destinations simultaneously
* Wire-OR superposition correct when random masks overlap on shared destinations
* Non-selected destinations remain quiet for every random configuration

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Generate random `CT_DST_SELECT` values constrained to two or more set bits per active source (wire-OR-only).
b. Write all active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back.
c. Drive random source pulses via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs.
d. Verify each pulse asserts every selected destination simultaneously and the routing mask is honored.
e. Where random masks overlap, verify the shared destination ORs the contributing sources; verify non-selected destinations stay idle.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_csr_chk` |Every randomized multicast mask reads back as programmed
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_route_chk` |All selected destinations assert per the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_or_chk` |Overlapping random sources OR correctly at shared destinations
|`ctm_rand_wire_or_only_nospur_chk` |No non-selected destination toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random wire-OR configuration multicasts correctly with correct superposition and no spurious destination toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-624-dtp_ctm_rand_p2p_only_test]]
===== 6.24 `dtp_ctm_rand_p2p_only_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM P2P only

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized single-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks restricted to point-to-point routing only
* Random request/acknowledge handshakes complete at exactly the selected destination
* No cross-route leakage between independent point-to-point routes
* Coverage of diverse single-bit destination positions across sources

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Generate random `CT_DST_SELECT` values constrained to exactly one set bit per active source (P2P-only).
b. Write all active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back to confirm a single destination bit is set.
c. Drive randomized handshakes on random source ports via the OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs.
d. Verify each request/ack completes only at its configured single destination.
e. Verify no other route observes the request (no cross-route leakage).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_csr_chk` |Every randomized P2P mask reads back with exactly one destination bit set
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_route_chk` |Each request/ack completes at the selected destination per the reference model
|`ctm_rand_p2p_only_noleak_chk` |No cross-route leakage between independent point-to-point routes
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random point-to-point configuration completes its handshake at exactly the configured destination with no cross-route leakage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-625-dtp_ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.25 `dtp_ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTM routes restricted to internal CLA sources targeting external CTP destinations
* Random single-bit and multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks and random trigger timing within the CLA-to-CTP class
* Selected CTP destinations match the random configuration; non-selected stay quiet
* Coverage of diverse CLA-source x CTP-destination route combinations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Randomize `CT_DST_SELECT` masks for CLA sources, with destination bits restricted to CTP ports.
b. Write the active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back.
c. Drive CLA triggers via the OCAH-local CTM BFM with randomized timing.
d. Observe CTP destinations via the OCAH-local CTP monitor and verify the selected CTP ports respond.
e. Sample the source x destination coverage of the exercised CLA-to-CTP combinations.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |Every randomized CLA-to-CTP mask reads back as programmed
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |Selected CTP destinations respond per the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No non-selected CTP destination toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random CLA-to-CTP configuration routes correctly to its selected CTP destinations with no spurious activity and full route-combination coverage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-626-dtp_ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.26 `dtp_ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM CTP to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTM routes restricted to external CTP sources targeting internal CLA destinations
* Random single-bit and multi-bit `CT_DST_SELECT` masks and random trigger timing within the CTP-to-CLA class
* Selected CLA destinations match the random configuration; non-selected stay quiet
* Coverage of diverse CTP-source x CLA-destination route combinations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations:
a. Randomize `CT_DST_SELECT` masks for CTP sources, with destination bits restricted to CLA channels.
b. Write the active `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` registers via `OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back.
c. Drive CTP triggers via the OCAH-local CTP BFM with randomized timing.
d. Observe CLA destinations via the OCAH-local CTM monitor and verify the selected CLA channels respond.
e. Sample the source x destination coverage of the exercised CTP-to-CLA combinations.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_csr_chk` |Every randomized CTP-to-CLA mask reads back as programmed
|`ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_route_chk` |Selected CLA destinations respond per the reference model across iterations
|`ctm_rand_ctp_to_cla_nospur_chk` |No non-selected CLA destination toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random CTP-to-CLA configuration routes correctly to its selected CLA destinations with no spurious activity and full route-combination coverage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-627-dtp_ctm_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test]]
===== 6.27 `dtp_ctm_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM BFM

*Objective*: CTM CSR deterministic sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic closure of CTM generated-regblock byte-enable and masked-write condition holes that random selection can miss
* Full-word, per-byte, and masked writes to `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` with readback after each
* Reserved bits read back as zero and are unaffected by writes
* Internal route decode tracks each programmed `CT_DST_SELECT` value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle and disable all CTM source routing.
. Sweep the CTM routing CSR fields deterministically via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
a. Apply full-word writes, then per-byte-enable writes, then masked writes to `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` to exercise each generated byte-enable and masked-write condition.
b. Read back after each write and compare the defined destination-select field against the expected value.
c. Confirm reserved bits remain zero and are not disturbed by adjacent writes.
. For representative swept values, confirm the internal route decode matches the programmed `CT_DST_SELECT` via the OCAH-local CTM monitor.
. Disable all CTM source routing before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_csr_chk` |Full-word, byte-enable, and masked-write readbacks match expected `CT_DST_SELECT` values
|`ctm_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_rsvd_chk` |Reserved bits read back zero and are undisturbed
|`ctm_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_route_chk` |Internal route decode tracks the programmed destination mask against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every byte-enable and masked-write condition in the CTM regblock is exercised and reads back correctly with reserved bits clear and route decode matching, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-628-dtp_ctm_rand_all_source_select_coverage_test]]
===== 6.28 `dtp_ctm_rand_all_source_select_coverage_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM BFM

*Objective*: CTM all-source select coverage

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic closure of per-source select mapping for every defined CTM source-select CSR (`CT_SRC[0].CONFIG_0` through `CT_SRC[25].CONFIG_0`)
* Single-destination, paired-destination, and inverted-destination `CT_DST_SELECT` masks written and read back at every source index
* No source index aliases another CTM source register
* Direct exercise of all generated CTM source-select mapping code in the default DTP configuration

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Assert power-on reset, bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle, and disable all CTM source routing.
. For every defined CTM source index, via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
a. Write a single-destination `CT_DST_SELECT` mask, read back, and compare the defined destination-select field.
b. Write a paired-destination mask, read back, and compare.
c. Write an inverted-destination mask, read back, and compare.
d. Confirm the written value affects only this source register and no neighboring `+CT_SRC[N]_CONFIG_0+` (no aliasing).
. Sample per-source select coverage so every `CT_SRC1..25_CONFIG_0` mapping is touched.
. Disable all CTM source routing again before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm_rand_all_source_select_coverage_csr_chk` |Every defined source-select CSR accepts and returns the single/paired/inverted mask
|`ctm_rand_all_source_select_coverage_alias_chk` |No source index aliases another CTM source register
|`ctm_rand_all_source_select_coverage_route_chk` |Per-source route decode matches the programmed mask against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every defined CTM source-select CSR is touched with single, paired, and inverted masks and reads back correctly with no aliasing across source indices, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

=== Counts

[cols=",",options="header",]

|Metric |Count
|Reference logical tests |72
|Scenario-expanded open-source test cases |147
|===

SMC fabric write/read scenarios map to the source modules recorded in the
scenario tables. GitHub issues should track implementation sequencing and any
temporary testlist aliases.

=== Regression Groups

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|===
|Group |Tests |Purpose
|`smoke` |`dtp_sanity_test`, `dtp_jtag_idcode_test` |Fast TAP bring-up gate
|`functional` |Smoke/JTAG2AXI tests from the public functional set |Legacy public functional regression
|`basic_jtag` |All 19 Smoke and Basic JTAG scenarios |TAP, instruction, reset, BSR-loopback, and TMP smoke coverage
|`debug_tdr` |12 TMP_STATUS, IC_RESET, DEBUG_CONTROL, and CAPS tests |Debug TDR coverage
|`all` |Smoke, Basic JTAG, JTAG2AXI single-op, and debug TDR tests |Aggregate public DTP regression
|`jtag2axi` |All JTAG2AXI write/read/error/robustness scenarios |AXI/AXI-Lite bridge coverage
|`scan` |STAP, 3DCR, and iJTAG scenarios |IEEE 1838 and IEEE 1687 coverage
|`xtrig` |XTRIG, CTP, and CTM scenarios |Cross-trigger and CSR coverage
|`nightly` |All 147 scenario-expanded tests |Full OCAH DTP regression
|===

=== Known Limitations

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|===
|Limitation |Impact |Resolution Path
|CTP/CTM generated CSR blocks are stubbed for Verilator |XTRIG CSR scenarios require non-stub CSR behavior |Replace stubs with Verilator-compatible CSR RTL or run these tests on a commercial simulator
|iJTAG and OCH cross-trigger protocols do not have established public cocotb VIP |No public protocol VIP identified |Implement OCAH-local DTP BFMs in `env/`
|Lifecycle is commonly held in all-one/full-debug mode for base bring-up |Security-gating scenarios require explicit lifecycle stimulus |Add directed lifecycle scenarios for security-gating coverage
|SV covergroups are not supported by Verilator |Functional coverage must be Python-side or commercial-sim |See `DTP_FCOV.adoc`
|===
